A.4.
Small-Step Operational Semantics of Computations

Standard computation rules

\displaystyle({\mathop{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}fun}}}\;(x\mathinner{:}X)\mapsto M)\,V

\displaystyle\leadsto M[V/x]

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}return}\,V)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N

\displaystyle\leadsto N[V/x]

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}match}\;(V,W)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}with}\;\{(x,y)\mapsto M\}

\displaystyle\leadsto M[V/x,W/y]

\displaystyle\mathllap{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}match}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inl}_{Y}\,V)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}with}\;\{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inl}\,x\mapsto M,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inr}\,y\mapsto N\}}

\displaystyle\leadsto M[V/x]

\displaystyle\mathllap{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}match}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inr}_{X}\,W)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}with}\;\{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inl}\,x\mapsto M,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}inr}\,y\mapsto N\}}

\displaystyle\leadsto N[W/y]

Algebraicity of signals, interrupt handlers, awaiting, and process creation

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,M))\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=M\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;y\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N_{1})\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N_{2}

\displaystyle\leadsto

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;y\,

\displaystyle r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=N_{1}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N_{2})

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}await}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}until}\;\langle y\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}await}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}until}\;\langle y\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=M\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,N_{1}))\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N_{2}

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=N_{1}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N_{2})

Commutativity of signals and process creation with interrupt handlers

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,N)

\displaystyle\leadsto

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,

\displaystyle\,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M_{1})\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M_{2},N)

\displaystyle\leadsto

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(

\displaystyle M_{2},\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M_{1})\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

Interrupt propagation

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}return}\,W)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}return}\,W

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,M))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,M))

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;W\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;p=M\big[V/x,R/r,W/s\big]\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,N)

where

R\mathrel{\overset{\text{\tiny def}}{=}}{\mathop{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}fun}}}\;(s^{\prime}\mathinner{:}S)\mapsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;s^{\prime}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}return}\,p

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;W\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N)

\displaystyle\leadsto

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto

\displaystyle\,M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;W\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(V,N)\hskip 9.24994pt{\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}(\mathsf{op}\neq\mathsf{op}^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}await}\;W\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}until}\;\langle x\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;M)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}await}\;W\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}until}\;\langle x\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,M)

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,N))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,N))

Awaiting a promise to be fulfilled

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}await}\;\langle V\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}until}\;\langle x\rangle\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;M

\displaystyle\leadsto M[V/x]

Unboxing a mobile value

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}unbox}\;[V]\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;[x]\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;M

\displaystyle\leadsto M[V/x]

Evaluation context rule

\displaystyle\frac{M\leadsto N}{\mathcal{E}[M]\leadsto\mathcal{E}[N]}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \@@ams@intertext{{where}}\cr\mathcal{E}\mathrel{\;{:}{:}{=}\ }&\,[~]\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}let}\;x=\mathcal{E}\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathcal{E})\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathcal{E})\\
\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}&\,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}_{S}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;\mathcal{E}\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,\mathcal{E})\end{aligned}

A.5.
Small-Step Operational Semantics of Processes

Individual computations

\displaystyle\frac{M\leadsto N}{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;M\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;N}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}

Signal hoisting

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,M))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;M)

Process creation

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}spawn}\;(M,N))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;M\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;N

Broadcasting

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P)\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}Q

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,Q))

\displaystyle P\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,Q)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P)\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}Q)

Interrupt propagation

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;M)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;(\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,M))

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}Q)

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P)\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,Q)

\displaystyle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,P))

\displaystyle\leadsto\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op^{\prime}}\,(W,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P))

Evaluation context rule

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{P\leadsto Q}{\mathcal{F}[P]\leadsto\mathcal{F}[Q]}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}

\displaystyle\begin{aligned} \@@ams@intertext{{where}}\cr\text{$\mathcal{F}$}\mathrel{\;{:}{:}{=}\ }&[~]\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathcal{F}\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}Q\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\!P\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}\mathcal{F}\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathcal{F})\mathrel{\,\;\big|\ \ \!}\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,\mathcal{F})\end{aligned}

A.6.
Process Type Reduction

\frac{}{X\mathbin{!!}(o,\iota)\rightsquigarrow X\mathbin{!!}(o,\iota)}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{}{X\mathbin{!!}({\mathsf{ops}}\,{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow\downarrow}}\,(o,\iota))\rightsquigarrow X\mathbin{!!}({\mathsf{ops}}\,{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow\downarrow}}\,({\mathsf{op}}\,{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\downarrow}}\,(o,\iota)))}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{C\rightsquigarrow C^{\prime}\hskip 9.24994ptD\rightsquigarrow D^{\prime}}{C\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}D\rightsquigarrow C^{\prime}\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}D^{\prime}}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\\
\frac{}{X\mathbin{!!}{(o,\iota)}\rightsquigarrow(X\mathbin{!!}{(o,\iota)})\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}(Y\mathbin{!!}{(o^{\prime},\iota^{\prime})})}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 9.24994pt\frac{}{Y\mathbin{!!}{(o^{\prime},\iota^{\prime})}\rightsquigarrow(X\mathbin{!!}{(o,\iota)})\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}(Y\mathbin{!!}{(o^{\prime},\iota^{\prime})})}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}

Result Forms

\frac{\mathsf{RunRes}\langle\Psi\cup\{p\}\,|\,N\rangle}{\mathsf{RunRes}\langle\Psi\,|\,\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}promise}\;(\mathsf{op}\;x\,r\,s\mapsto M)\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}@}\;V\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}as}\;p\;\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}in}\;N\rangle}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\\

\textbf{Processes}\\
\frac{\mathsf{ProcRes}\langle P\rangle}{\mathsf{ProcRes}\langle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}\uparrow}\,\mathsf{op}\,(V,P)\rangle}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 18.49988pt\frac{\mathsf{ParRes}\langle P\rangle}{\mathsf{ProcRes}\langle P\rangle}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 18.49988pt\frac{\mathsf{RunRes}\langle\emptyset\,|\,M\rangle}{\mathsf{ParRes}\langle\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}run}\;M\rangle}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}\hskip 18.49988pt\frac{\mathsf{ParRes}\langle P\rangle\hskip 9.24994pt\mathsf{ParRes}\langle Q\rangle}{\mathsf{ParRes}\langle P\mathbin{\mathsf{\color[rgb]{0,0,0.5}||}}Q\rangle}\hskip 9.24994pt\textsc{Lab={\color[rgb]{0.5,0.5,0.5}}}
